Cara memasangnya<br>* Buka folder proyek `moodora`.<br>* Buka folder `css`, lalu buat file `style.css`.<br>* Salin seluruh kode CSS di atas.<br>* Simpan file tersebut.<br>* Buka `index.html` melalui browser untuk melihat tampilan awal.<br>Kalau ada fitur yang belum bereaksi saat diklik, itu wajar untuk tahap ini karena kita baru menyelesaikan HTML dan CSS. Interaksi menu mobile, kuesioner 10 pertanyaan, penyimpanan jurnal, grafik mood, Mood Garden, cerita interaktif, dan fitur lainnya akan diaktifkan di JavaScript.<br>Berikutnya: `js/script.js` — ini bagian yang akan membuat Moodora benar-benar hidup, termasuk logika refleksi yang menyesuaikan jawaban pengguna, penyimpanan lokal, animasi interaktif, dan pengelolaan data dengan lebih aman. | code css ini sudah benar tetapi belum sempat saya cek dan langsung menuju ke Script JS | Chatgpt |
| 3 | sekarang js nya | Gas! 🔥 Sekarang kita lanjut ke JavaScript (`script.js`) untuk Moodora. Bagian ini yang bikin fitur website kita benar-benar berfungsi, bukan cuma bagus tampilannya.<br>JavaScript ini dirancang untuk dipasangkan dengan `index.html` dan `style.css` yang sudah kamu buat. Fitur utamanya meliputi:<br>* Mood Check-in: pilih suasana hati, intensitas, dan faktor yang memengaruhinya.<br>* Self-check: kuesioner 10 pertanyaan dengan progress bar, tombol kembali/lanjut, dan hasil refleksi.<br>* Calm Corner: latihan pernapasan dengan animasi dan hitung mundur.<br>* Daily Journal: tulis dan simpan catatan secara lokal di perangkat.<br>* Small Steps: rekomendasi langkah kecil sesuai kondisi yang dipilih.<br>* Privasi: data disimpan di browser menggunakan `localStorage`, tanpa database.<br>Catatan penting: hasil kuesioner Moodora adalah alat refleksi, bukan diagnosis medis. Pertanyaan tentang keselamatan diri akan ditangani secara khusus dan tidak digabungkan ke skor.<br>Buat file `js/script.js`, lalu masukkan kode lengkap berikut. Aku buat dengan pengecekan elemen supaya fitur yang belum memiliki komponen di HTML tidak langsung menyebabkan seluruh JavaScript berhenti.<br>```javascript<br>/* =========================================================<br>MOODORA — YOUR FEELINGS, YOUR SPACE<br>File: js/script.js<br>Fitur:<br>- Navigasi responsif<br>- Mood Check-in<br>- Kuesioner refleksi 10 pertanyaan<br>- Calm Corner (latihan pernapasan)<br>- Daily Journal<br>- Small Steps<br>- Penyimpanan lokal dan penghapusan data<br>- Notifikasi dan animasi sederhana<br>========================================================= */<br>"use strict";<br>/* =========================================================<br>1. KONFIGURASI DAN UTILITAS<br>========================================================= */<br>const STORAGE_KEYS = {<br>moods: "moodora_moods",<br>journals: "moodora_journals",<br>habits: "moodora_habits",<br>questionnaire: "moodora_questionnaire"<br>};<br>const $ = (selector, parent = document) =><br>parent.querySelector(selector);<br>const $$ = (selector, parent = document) =><br>[...parent.querySelectorAll(selector)];<br>function readStorage(key, fallback = []) {<br>try {<br>const value = localStorage.getItem(key);<br>return value ? JSON.parse(value) : fallback;<br>} catch (error) {<br>console.warn(`Gagal membaca data ${key}:`, error);<br>return fallback;<br>}<br>}<br>function saveStorage(key, value) {<br>try {<br>localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));<br>return true;<br>} catch (error) {<br>console.warn(`Gagal menyimpan data ${key}:`, error);<br>showToast("Data tidak dapat disimpan di browser ini.");<br>return false;<br>}<br>}<br>function escapeHTML(value) {<br>return String(value ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, character => ({<br>"&": "&amp;",<br>"<": "&lt;",<br>">": "&gt;",<br>'"': "&quot;",<br>"'": "&#039;"<br>})[character]);<br>}<br>function todayString() {<br>const now = new Date();<br>const offset = now.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000;<br>return new Date(now.getTime() - offset).toISOString().slice(0, 10);<br>}<br>function formatDate(dateString) {<br>const date = new Date(`${dateString}T00:00:00`);<br>if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateString;<br>return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("id-ID", {<br>day: "numeric",<br>month: "long",<br>year: "numeric"<br>}).format(date);<br>}<br>function showToast(message) {<br>let container = $("#toast-container");<br>if (!container) {<br>container = document.createElement("div");<br>container.id = "toast-container";<br>container.setAttribute("aria-live", "polite");<br>container.style.cssText = `<br>position: fixed;<br>right: 20px;<br>bottom: 20px;<br>z-index: 9999;<br>display: grid;<br>gap: 10px;<br>max-width: min(360px, calc(100vw - 40px));<br>`;<br>document.body.appendChild(container);<br>}<br>const toast = document.createElement("div");<br>toast.textContent = message;<br>toast.style.cssText = `<br>padding: 13px 17px;<br>border-radius: 14px;<br>color: #ffffff;<br>background: #303447;<br>box-shadow: 0 8px 28px rgba(0,0,0,.16);<br>font-size: 14px;<br>line-height: 1.5;<br>animation: moodoraToastIn .25s ease;<br>`;<br>container.appendChild(toast);<br>window.setTimeout(() => {<br>toast.style.opacity = "0";<br>toast.style.transform = "translateY(8px)";<br>toast.style.transition = "opacity .2s, transform .2s";<br>window.setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 250);<br>}, 3000);<br>}<br>function scrollToElement(element) {<br>if (!element) return;<br>element.scrollIntoView({<br>behavior: window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches<br>? "auto"<br>: "smooth",<br>block: "start"<br>});<br>}<br>function bindClick(selector, callback) {<br>$$(selector).forEach(element => {<br>element.addEventListener("click", callback);<br>});<br>}<br>/* =========================================================<br>2. NAVIGASI RESPONSIF<br>========================================================= */<br>function initNavigation() {<br>const menuToggle = $("#menu-toggle");<br>const navMenu = $("#nav-menu") \|\| $("#primary-nav");<br>if (menuToggle && navMenu) {<br>menuToggle.addEventListener("click", () => {<br>const isOpen = navMenu.classList.toggle("is-open");<br>menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));<br>menuToggle.setAttribute(<br>"aria-label",<br>isOpen ? "Tutup menu navigasi" : "Buka menu navigasi"<br>);<br>});<br>$$(".nav-link, .nav-cta", navMenu).forEach(link => {<br>link.addEventListener("click", () => {<br>navMenu.classList.remove("is-open");<br>menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");<br>});<br>});<br>document.addEventListener("keydown", event => {<br>if (event.key === "Escape") {<br>navMenu.classList.remove("is-open");<br>menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");<br>}<br>});<br>}<br>// Tandai menu sesuai bagian yang sedang terlihat.<br>const navLinks = $$(".nav-link");<br>if ("IntersectionObserver" in window && navLinks.length) {<br>const sections = navLinks<br>.map(link => {<br>const href = link.getAttribute("href");<br>return href?.startsWith("#") ? $(href) : null;<br>})<br>.filter(Boolean);<br>const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {<br>entries.forEach(entry => {<br>if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;<br>navLinks.forEach(link => {<br>const active = link.getAttribute("href") === `#${entry.target.id}`;<br>link.classList.toggle("active", active);<br>if (active) {<br>link.setAttribute("aria-current", "location");<br>} else {<br>link.removeAttribute("aria-current");<br>}<br>});<br>});<br>}, {<br>rootMargin: "-25% 0px -60% 0px"<br>});<br>sections.forEach(section => observer.observe(section));<br>}<br>}<br>/* =========================================================<br>3. MOOD CHECK-IN<br>========================================================= */<br>const MOOD_INFO = {<br>happy: {<br>label: "Bahagia",<br>emoji: "😊",<br>message: "Senang rasanya kalau ada hal yang membuatmu bahagia."<br>},<br>calm: {<br>label: "Tenang",<br>emoji: "😌",<br>message: "Nikmati momen tenang ini tanpa perlu terburu-buru."<br>},<br>okay: {<br>label: "Biasa saja",<br>emoji: "🙂",<br>message: "Tidak harus selalu merasa luar biasa. Biasa saja juga tidak apa-apa."<br>},<br>sad: {<br>label: "Sedih",<br>emoji: "😔",<br>message: "Kamu boleh memberi ruang untuk perasaanmu hari ini."<br>},<br>anxious: {<br>label: "Cemas",<br>emoji: "😰",<br>message: "Coba hadapi semuanya satu langkah kecil pada satu waktu."<br>},<br>angry: {<br>label: "Kesal",<br>emoji: "😤",<br>message: "Ambil jeda sebelum merespons. Perasaanmu layak dipahami."<br>},<br>tired: {<br>label: "Lelah",<br>emoji: "😴",<br>message: "Istirahat juga bagian dari proses untuk terus bertumbuh."<br>}<br>};<br>function initMoodCheckIn() {<br>const form = $("#mood-form") \|\| $(".mood-form");<br>const intensity = $("#mood-intensity");<br>const result = $("#mood-result");<br>const intensityValue = $("#mood-intensity-value");<br>if (intensity && intensityValue) {<br>intensity.addEventListener("input", () => {<br>intensityValue.textContent = intensity.value;<br>});<br>}<br>if (!form) return;<br>form.addEventListener("submit", event => {<br>event.preventDefault();<br>const selectedMood = $(<br>'input[name="mood"]:checked, input[name="mood-choice"]:checked, ' +<br>'input[name="feeling"]:checked',<br>form<br>);<br>const moodValue =<br>selectedMood?.value \|\|<br>form.querySelector("[data-mood].selected")?.dataset.mood \|\|<br>form.querySelector("[data-mood].is-selected")?.dataset.mood;<br>if (!moodValue) {<br>showToast("Pilih suasana hati yang paling menggambarkan dirimu.");<br>return;<br>}<br>const mood = MOOD_INFO[moodValue] \|\| {<br>label: moodValue,<br>emoji: "💭",<br>message: "Terima kasih sudah meluangkan waktu untuk memahami dirimu."<br>};<br>const factors = $$(<br>'input[name="factor"]:checked, input[name="mood-factor"]:checked, ' +<br>'input[name="trigger"]:checked',<br>form<br>).map(input => input.value);<br>const entry = {<br>id: Date.now(),<br>date: todayString(),<br>mood: moodValue,<br>label: mood.label,<br>intensity: Number(intensity?.value \|\| 3),<br>factors,<br>createdAt: new Date().toISOString()<br>};<br>const moods = readStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.moods, []);<br>moods.unshift(entry);<br>if (!saveStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.moods, moods)) return;<br>if (result) {<br>result.textContent = `${mood.emoji} ${mood.message}`;<br>result.hidden = false;<br>}<br>renderMoodHistory();<br>renderMoodInsights();<br>showToast("Check-in berhasil disimpan. Terima kasih sudah jujur pada diri sendiri.");<br>form.reset();<br>if (intensity) intensity.value = "3";<br>if (intensityValue) intensityValue.textContent = "3";<br>});<br>// Memilih mood melalui kartu atau tombol data-mood.<br>$$("[data-mood]").forEach(button => {<br>button.addEventListener("click", () => {<br>const moodValue = button.dataset.mood;<br>const radio = $(<br>`input[name="mood"][value="${CSS.escape(moodValue)}"], ` +<br>`input[name="mood-choice"][value="${CSS.escape(moodValue)}"], ` +<br>`input[name="feeling"][value="${CSS.escape(moodValue)}"]`,<br>form \|\| document<br>);<br>if (radio) {<br>radio.checked = true;<br>radio.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));<br>}<br>$$("[data-mood]").forEach(item => {<br>item.classList.remove("selected", "is-selected");<br>item.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");<br>});<br>button.classList.add("selected");<br>button.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");<br>});<br>});<br>renderMoodHistory();<br>renderMoodInsights();<br>}<br>function renderMoodHistory() {<br>const container = $("#mood-history");<br>if (!container) return;<br>const moods = readStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.moods, []).slice(0, 7);<br>if (!moods.length) {<br>container.textContent = "Catatan suasana hatimu akan muncul di sini.";<br>return;<br>}<br>container.innerHTML = moods.map(entry => {<br>const mood = MOOD_INFO[entry.mood] \|\| {<br>emoji: "💭",<br>label: entry.label \|\| entry.mood<br>};<br>return `<br><article class="mood-history-item"><br><span aria-hidden="true">${mood.emoji}</span><br><div><br><strong>${escapeHTML(mood.label)}</strong><br><p>${escapeHTML(formatDate(entry.date))}</p><br><small>Intensitas: ${Number(entry.intensity)}/5</small><br></div><br></article><br>`;<br>}).join("");<br>}<br>function renderMoodInsights() {<br>const container = $("#mood-insights");<br>if (!container) return;<br>const moods = readStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.moods, []).slice(0, 7);<br>if (!moods.length) {<br>container.textContent =<br>"Lakukan beberapa check-in untuk mulai melihat pola suasana hatimu.";<br>return;<br>}<br>const counts = {};<br>moods.forEach(entry => {<br>counts[entry.mood] = (counts[entry.mood] \|\| 0) + 1;<br>});<br>const topMood = Object.keys(counts).sort(<br>(a, b) => counts[b] - counts[a]<br>)[0];<br>const info = MOOD_INFO[topMood];<br>container.textContent = info<br>? `Dalam ${moods.length} check-in terakhir, suasana hati yang paling sering kamu catat adalah ${info.label.toLowerCase()}. KUESIONER REFLEKSI DIRI<br>========================================================= */<br>const QUESTIONS = [<br>{<br>text: "Dalam dua minggu terakhir, seberapa sering kamu kehilangan minat pada hal yang biasanya kamu sukai?",<br>theme: "interest"<br>},<br>{<br>text: "Seberapa sering kamu merasa sedih, kosong, atau kehilangan harapan?",<br>theme: "sadness"<br>},<br>{<br>text: "Seberapa sering SMALL STEPS — KEBIASAAN KECIL<br>========================================================= */<br>const DEFAULT_HABITS = [<br>{ id: "water", text: "Minum air putih", done: false },<br>{ id: "break", text: "Istirahat sebentar dari layar", done: false },<br>{ id: "outside", text: "Mencari udara segar", done: false },<br>{ id: "talk", text: "Menghubungi seseorang yang dipercaya", done: false },<br>{ id: "journal", text: "Menulis satu hal yang dirasakan", done: false }<br>];<br>function initSmallSteps() {<br>const container = $("#habit-list");<br>const resetButton = $("#reset-habits");<br>if (!container) return;<br>const stored = readStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.habits, null);<br>if (!stored \|\| stored.date !== todayString()) {<br>saveStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.habits, {<br>date: todayString(),<br>items: DEFAULT_HABITS.map(item => ({ ...item }))<br>});<br>}<br>renderHabits();<br>resetButton?.addEventListener("click", () => {<br>saveStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.habits, {<br>date: todayString(),<br>items: DEFAULT_HABITS.map(item => ({ ...item }))<br>});<br>renderHabits();<br>showToast("Daftar Small Steps hari ini sudah diatur ulang.");<br>});<br>}<br>function renderHabits() {<br>const container = $("#habit-list");<br>if (!container) return;<br>let data = readStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.habits, null);<br>if (!data \|\| !Array.isArray(data.items)) {<br>data = {<br>date: todayString(),<br>items: DEFAULT_HABITS.map(item => ({ ...item }))<br>};<br>}<br>if (data.date !== todayString()) {<br>data = {<br>date: todayString(),<br>items: DEFAULT_HABITS.map(item => ({ ...item }))<br>};<br>saveStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.habits, data);<br>}<br>const completed = data.items.filter(item => item.done).length;<br>const percent = Math.round((completed / Math.max(data.items.length, 1)) * 100);<br>container.innerHTML = data.items.map(item => `<br><label class="habit-card"><br><input<br>type="checkbox"<br>data-habit-id="${escapeHTML(item.id)}"<br>${item.done ? " +<br>"Data yang dihapus tidak bisa dikembalikan."<br>);<br>if (!confirmed) return;<br>Object.values(STORAGE_KEYS).forEach(key => {<br>localStorage.removeItem(key);<br>});<br>// Reset tampilan yang menampilkan data lokal.<br>renderMoodHistory();<br>renderMoodInsights();<br>renderJournals();<br>const habitList = $("#habit-list");<br>if (habitList) {<br>saveStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.habits, {<br>date: todayString(),<br>items: DEFAULT_HABITS.map(item => ({ ...item }))<br>});<br>renderHabits();<br>}<br>const moodResult = $("#mood-result");<br>if (moodResult) {<br>moodResult.textContent = "";<br>moodResult.hidden = true;<br>}<br>const questionnaireForm = $("#questionnaire-form");<br>const questionnaireResult = $("#reflection-result");<br>const startQuestionnaire = $("#start-questionnaire");<br>if (questionnaireForm) questionnaireForm.hidden = true;<br>if (questionnaireResult) questionnaireResult.hidden = true;<br>if (startQuestionnaire) startQuestionnaire.hidden = false;<br>$("#safety-support-message")?.remove();<br>const journalForm = $("#journal-form");<br>journalForm?.reset();<br>const editId = $("#journal-edit-id");<br>if (editId) editId.value = "";<br>showToast("Data Moodora di browser ini telah dihapus.");<br>});<br>}<br>/* =========================================================<br>10. EFEK MASUK SAAT SCROLL<br>========================================================= */<br>function initRevealAnimations() {<br>const elements = $$(".reveal-on-scroll");<br>if (!elements.length \|\| !("IntersectionObserver" in window)) {<br>elements.forEach(element => element.classList.add("is-visible"));<br>return;<br>}<br>const reduceMotion = window.matchMedia(<br>"(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)"<br>).matches;<br>if (reduceMotion) {<br>elements.forEach(element => element.classList.add("is-visible"));<br>return;<br>}<br>const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {<br>entries.forEach(entry => {<br>if (entry.isIntersecting) {<br>entry.target.classList.add("is-visible");<br>observer.unobserve(entry.target);<br>}<br>});<br>}, {<br>threshold: 0.12<br>});<br>elements.forEach(element => observer.observe(element));<br>}<br>/* =========================================================<br>11. INISIALISASI MOODORA<br>========================================================= */<br>function initMoodora() {<br>initNavigation();<br>initMoodCheckIn();<br>initQuestionnaire();<br>initBreathing();<br>initJournal();<br>initSmallSteps();<br>initGeneralInteractions();<br>initPrivacy();<br>initRevealAnimations();<br>console.info("Moodora berhasil diinisialisasi ✨");<br>}<br>if (document.readyState === "loading") {<br>document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initMoodora);<br>} else {<br>initMoodora();<br>}<br>```<br>### Cara memasangnya<br>1\. Pastikan struktur folder benar<br>Nama folder dan file harus sesuai dengan yang dipanggil oleh HTML.<br>```text<br>moodora/<br>├── index.html<br>├── css/<br>│ └── style.css<br>└── js/<br>└── script.js<br>```<br>2\. Hubungkan JavaScript ke HTML<br>Pastikan sebelum tag penutup `</body>` ada baris ini:<br>```html<br><script src="js/script.js"></script><br>```<br>3\. Tes fitur satu per satu<br>* Coba simpan mood check-in.<br>* Mulai kuesioner dan jawab sampai selesai.<br>* Jalankan lalu hentikan latihan pernapasan.<br>* Simpan, edit, dan hapus jurnal.<br>* Centang Small Steps.<br>* Uji tombol hapus data setelah mencoba fitur lain.<br>Catatan integrasi: JavaScript ini mengharapkan beberapa ID tertentu, misalnya `#mood-form`, `#journal-form`, `#breathing-start`, dan `#habit-list`. Kalau nama elemen di HTML kamu berbeda, fitur yang terkait perlu disesuaikan. Selain itu, data `localStorage` hanya tersimpan pada browser dan perangkat yang digunakan, bukan otomatis tersinkronisasi ke perangkat lain.<br>Kalau setelah dipasang ada fitur yang belum berjalan, kirim screenshot error dari Console browser atau potongan HTML bagian tersebut. Kita cocokkan JavaScript-nya dengan HTML dan CSS Moodora yang kamu punya. | setelah saya mendapatkan semua code HTML, CSS, dan JS saya memulai proses pembuatan pada Github | Chatgpt |

TAHAP 3 : Pemecahan Masalah & Penanganan Galat

| No | Prompt | Validasi | Modifikasi | Keterangan |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | ini output intensitas mood nya tetap 3/5 padahal udah diganti ganti, lalu animasi di awal nya kurang 3d dan interaktif, warnanya juga monoton full putih padahal kan di percakapan tadi warna nya agak gelap dan banyak variasi nya | Nah, dari screenshot yang lo kirim, gue lihat ada 3 hal yang perlu kita benahi di Moodora.<br>* Intensitas mood selalu 3/5. Kemungkinan nilai slider belum ikut dimasukkan ke hasil, atau tampilan hasil masih memakai angka default. Di JavaScript sebelumnya, nilai intensitas sebenarnya dibaca saat form dikirim, tetapi belum ada kode yang secara khusus menampilkan hasil dengan format `3/5`. Kita perlu memastikan nilai slider, data yang disimpan, dan hasil yang ditampilkan selalu sinkron.<br>* Animasi halaman awal masih terasa datar. Kita bisa bikin elemen hero terasa lebih hidup dengan efek kedalaman 3D, orb yang bergerak, kartu yang sedikit miring mengikuti kursor, serta animasi masuk yang halus.<br>* Warna terlalu putih dan monoton. Kita ubah ke gaya dark dreamy: navy gelap sebagai dasar, perpaduan ungu dan biru elektrik, aksen cyan, serta kartu dengan efek kaca. Jadi tetap modern dan cocok untuk semua gender, bukan sekadar mengganti putih menjadi hitam.<br>Kita perbaiki satu per satu supaya fitur yang sudah berjalan tidak ikut rusak.<br>### 1\. Perbaiki intensitas mood supaya nilainya benar<br>Buka `js/script.js`. Cari fungsi `initMoodCheckIn()`, lalu ganti seluruh fungsi tersebut dengan versi ini. Fungsi lain di file jangan dihapus.<br>```javascript<br>function initMoodCheckIn() {<br>const form = document.querySelector("#mood-form")<br>\|\| document.querySelector(".mood-form");<br>const intensity = document.querySelector("#mood-intensity");<br>const result = document.querySelector("#mood-result");<br>// Tampilkan nilai slider secara langsung.<br>let intensityValue = document.querySelector("#mood-intensity-value");<br>if (intensity && !intensityValue) {<br>intensityValue = document.createElement("span");<br>intensityValue.id = "mood-intensity-value";<br>intensityValue.className = "intensity-value";<br>intensity.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", intensityValue);<br>}<br>function updateIntensity() {<br>if (!intensity) return;<br>const value = Number(intensity.value);<br>if (intensityValue) {<br>intensityValue.textContent = `${value}/5`;<br>}<br>intensity.setAttribute("aria-valuetext", `${value} dari 5`);<br>}<br>if (intensity) {<br>updateIntensity();<br>intensity.addEventListener("input", updateIntensity);<br>intensity.addEventListener("change", updateIntensity);<br>}<br>if (!form) return;<br>form.addEventListener("submit", event => {<br>event.preventDefault();<br>const selectedMood = form.querySelector(<br>'input[name="mood"]:checked, ' +<br>'input[name="mood-choice"]:checked, ' +<br>'input[name="feeling"]:checked'<br>);<br>const selectedCard = form.querySelector(<br>"[data-mood].selected, [data-mood].is-selected"<br>);<br>const moodValue = selectedMood?.value \|\| selectedCard?.dataset.mood;<br>if (!moodValue) {<br>showToast("Pilih suasana hati kamu terlebih dahulu.");<br>return;<br>}<br>// Baca nilai terbaru ketika tombol simpan ditekan.<br>const intensityNumber = Number(intensity?.value ?? 3);<br>const mood = MOOD_INFO[moodValue] \|\| {<br>label: moodValue,<br>emoji: "💭",<br>message: "Terima kasih sudah mengenali perasaanmu."<br>};<br>const factors = [<br>...form.querySelectorAll(<br>'input[name="factor"]:checked, ' +<br>'input[name="mood-factor"]:checked, ' +<br>'input[name="trigger"]:checked'<br>)<br>].map(input => input.value);<br>const entry = {<br>id: Date.now(),<br>date: todayString(),<br>mood: moodValue,<br>label: mood.label,<br>intensity: intensityNumber,<br>factors,<br>createdAt: new Date().toISOString()<br>};<br>const moods = readStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.moods, []);<br>moods.unshift(entry);<br>if (!saveStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.moods, moods)) return;<br>// Hasil menunjukkan nilai yang benar-benar dipilih.<br>if (result) {<br>result.replaceChildren();<br>const heading = document.createElement("strong");<br>heading.textContent =<br>`${mood.emoji} ${mood.label} — Intensitas ${intensityNumber}/5`;<br>const description = document.createElement("p");<br>description.textContent = mood.message;<br>result.append(heading, description);<br>result.hidden = false;<br>}<br>renderMoodHistory();<br>renderMoodInsights();<br>showToast(`Mood tersimpan dengan intensitas ${intensityNumber}/5.`);<br>});<br>renderMoodHistory();<br>renderMoodInsights();<br>}<br>```<br>Penting: kalau HTML lo belum punya elemen `id="mood-intensity"` atau `id="mood-result"`, nama ID tersebut perlu disamakan dengan HTML. Kode di atas mempertahankan slider setelah disimpan agar lo bisa melihat nilai yang dipilih, tidak langsung mengembalikannya ke angka 3.<br>### 2\. Bikin Moodora lebih gelap dan punya kedalaman visual<br>Tambahkan kode berikut di bagian paling bawah `css/style.css`. Dengan begitu, gaya baru ini akan menimpa banyak aturan warna lama.<br>```css<br>/* ==========================================<br>MOODORA — DARK DREAMY THEME<br>Tambahkan di bagian paling bawah style.css<br>========================================== */<br>:root {<br>color-scheme: dark;<br>--mood-bg: #0b1020;<br>--mood-surface: #131b31;<br>--mood-surface-2: #1a2541;<br>--mood-border: rgba(164, 181, 255, 0.17);<br>--mood-text: #edf1ff;<br>--mood-muted: #a6b2d0;<br>--mood-blue: #8ba7ff;<br>--mood-purple: #b39aff;<br>--mood-cyan: #72e4ed;<br>--mood-radius: 22px;<br>}<br>html {<br>scroll-behavior: smooth;<br>scroll-padding-top: 100px;<br>}<br>body {<br>color: var(--mood-text);<br>background:<br>radial-gradient(ellipse at 15% 5%, #27346a55, transparent 35%),<br>radial-gradient(ellipse at 90% 30%, #572c7d30, transparent 32%),<br>var(--mood-bg);<br>background-attachment: fixed;<br>}<br>body,<br>button,<br>input,<br>textarea,<br>select {<br>font-family: inherit;<br>}<br>p,<br>label,<br>small {<br>color: var(--mood-muted);<br>}<br>h1,<br>h2,<br>h3,<br>h4,<br>strong,<br>legend {<br>color: var(--mood-text);<br>}<br>/* Navbar gelap dengan efek kaca */<br>header,<br>nav,<br>.navbar,<br>.site-header {<br>background: rgba(11, 16, 32, 0.82);<br>border-color: var(--mood-border);<br>}<br>header,<br>.navbar,<br>.site-header {<br>backdrop-filter: blur(18px);<br>-webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(18px);<br>}<br>a {<br>color: var(--mood-blue);<br>transition: color 0.25s ease;<br>}<br>a:hover {<br>color: var(--mood-cyan);<br>}<br>/* Kartu dan panel */<br>.card,<br>.mood-card,<br>.feature-card,<br>.journal-entry,<br>.question-card,<br>.habit-card,<br>[class*="card"] {<br>border-color: var(--mood-border);<br>}<br>.card,<br>.feature-card,<br>.journal-entry,<br>.question-card {<br>background: linear-gradient(<br>145deg,<br>rgba(27, 39, 68, 0.94),<br>rgba(17, 25, 45, 0.96)<br>);<br>border: 1px solid var(--mood-border);<br>border-radius: var(--mood-radius);<br>box-shadow: 0 16px 45px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.18);<br>}<br>.feature-card,<br>.mood-card {<br>transition:<br>transform 0.3s ease,<br>border-color 0.3s ease,<br>box-shadow 0.3s ease;<br>}<br>.feature-card:hover,<br>.mood-card:hover {<br>transform: translateY(-5px);<br>border-color: rgba(139, 167, 255, 0.55);<br>box-shadow: 0 18px 45px rgba(69, 91, 190, 0.18);<br>}<br>/* Mood pilihan */<br>[data-mood],<br>.answer-option,<br>.habit-card {<br>cursor: pointer;<br>}<br>[data-mood] {<br>background: rgba(25, 36, 62, 0.9);<br>border: 1px solid var(--mood-border);<br>border-radius: 18px;<br>transition: 0.25s ease;<br>}<br>[data-mood]:hover,<br>[data-mood].selected,<br>[data-mood].is-selected {<br>background: linear-gradient(135deg, #354b85, #413367);<br>border-color: var(--mood-blue);<br>box-shadow: 0 0 24px rgba(139, 167, 255, 0.15);<br>}<br>/* Input dan slider */<br>input:not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]):not([type="range"]),<br>textarea,<br>select {<br>color: var(--mood-text);<br>background: #10182c;<br>border: 1px solid var(--mood-border);<br>border-radius: 12px;<br>}<br>input[type="range"] {<br>accent-color: var(--mood-blue);<br>cursor: pointer;<br>}<br>input[type="checkbox"],<br>input[type="radio"] {<br>accent-color: var(--mood-blue);<br>}<br>.intensity-value {<br>display: inline-block;<br>margin: 10px 0;<br>padding: 6px 12px;<br>color: var(--mood-cyan);<br>background: rgba(114, 228, 237, 0.09);<br>border: 1px solid rgba(114, 228, 237, 0.2);<br>border-radius: 999px;<br>font-weight: 700;<br>}<br>/* Tombol utama */<br>button,<br>.btn-primary,<br>.nav-cta {<br>transition:<br>transform 0.2s ease,<br>box-shadow 0.2s ease,<br>background 0.2s ease;<br>}<br>button:hover,<br>.btn-primary:hover,<br>.nav-cta:hover {<br>transform: translateY(-2px);<br>}<br>button:focus-visible,<br>a:focus-visible,<br>input:focus-visible,<br>textarea:focus-visible {<br>outline: 3px solid var(--mood-cyan);<br>outline-offset: 3px;<br>}<br>button:disabled {<br>opacity: 0.55;<br>cursor: not-allowed;<br>transform: none;<br>}<br>/* Hero: kedalaman visual dan cahaya */<br>.hero {<br>position: relative;<br>isolation: isolate;<br>overflow: hidden;<br>background:<br>radial-gradient(ellipse at 75% 40%, #6e5bd42b, transparent 35%),<br>radial-gradient(ellipse at 15% 85%, #338da52b, transparent 32%);<br>}<br>.hero::before,<br>.hero::after {<br>content: "";<br>position: absolute;<br>z-index: -1;<br>width: 280px;<br>aspect-ratio: 1;<br>border-radius: 50%;<br>filter: blur(65px);<br>opacity: 0.45;<br>pointer-events: none;<br>animation: moodoraFloat 9s ease-in-out infinite alternate;<br>}<br>.hero::before {<br>top: 3%;<br>right: 8%;<br>background: #6c68ff;<br>}<br>.hero::after {<br>bottom: -15%;<br>left: 18%;<br>background: #29c8d9;<br>animation-delay: -4s;<br>}<br>.hero h1 {<br>letter-spacing: -0.045em;<br>text-wrap: balance;<br>}<br>.hero h1 span,<br>.gradient-text {<br>color: transparent;<br>background: linear-gradient(<br>100deg,<br>#9cb5ff,<br>#c4a5ff 52%,<br>#78e9ed<br>);<br>background-clip: text;<br>-webkit-background-clip: text;<br>}<br>/* Tambahkan class hero-visual pada area ilustrasi hero */<br>.hero-visual {<br>position: relative;<br>perspective: 1000px;<br>transform-style: preserve-3d;<br>}<br>.hero-visual .hero-card {<br>position: relative;<br>transform: rotateY(-8deg) rotateX(4deg);<br>transform-style: preserve-3d;<br>background: linear-gradient(<br>145deg,<br>rgba(51, 66, 111, 0.86),<br>rgba(24, 30, 57, 0.96)<br>);<br>border: 1px solid rgba(190, 205, 255, 0.25);<br>border-radius: 28px;<br>box-shadow:<br>0 35px 80px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35),<br>inset 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);<br>animation: moodoraCardFloat 6s ease-in-out infinite;<br>}<br>.hero-visual .orb {<br>position: absolute;<br>width: 74px;<br>aspect-ratio: 1;<br>border-radius: 50%;<br>background: radial-gradient(<br>circle at 30% 25%,<br>#e3e8ff,<br>#9c9aff 32%,<br>#6157bf 72%,<br>#37336e<br>);<br>box-shadow:<br>inset -8px -10px 18px #17183970,<br>0 0 38px #8e91ff55;<br>animation: moodoraOrbFloat 5s ease-in-out infinite;<br>}<br>.hero-visual .orb:nth-child(2) {<br>width: 38px;<br>animation-delay: -2s;<br>}<br>.hero-visual .orb:nth-child(3) {<br>width: 22px;<br>animation-delay: -3.5s;<br>}<br>/* Progress bar */<br>#question-progress,<br>#steps-progress-fill,<br>#steps-progress-bar {<br>background-color: rgba(139, 167, 255, 0.16);<br>border-radius: 999px;<br>overflow: hidden;<br>}<br>#question-progress-fill,<br>#steps-progress-fill,<br>#steps-progress-bar {<br>background-image: linear-gradient(90deg, #8ba7ff, #b39aff, #72e4ed);<br>transition: width 0.35s ease;<br>}<br>/* Hasil dan notifikasi */<br>#mood-result,<br>#reflection-result,<br>#safety-support-message {<br>color: var(--mood-text);<br>background: linear-gradient(135deg, #202f50, #292344);<br>border: 1px solid var(--mood-border);<br>border-radius: 18px;<br>padding: 20px;<br>}<br>#safety-support-message {<br>border-color: rgba(255, 178, 143, 0.5);<br>}<br>@keyframes moodoraFloat {<br>from {<br>transform: translate3d(-10px, -10px, 0) scale(0.95);<br>}<br>to {<br>transform: translate3d(25px, 20px, 0) scale(1.12);<br>}<br>}<br>@keyframes moodoraCardFloat {<br>0%, 100% {<br>transform: rotateY(-8deg) rotateX(4deg) translateY(0);<br>}<br>50% {<br>transform: rotateY(-4deg) rotateX(2deg) translateY(-9px);<br>}<br>}<br>@keyframes moodoraOrbFloat {<br>0%, 100% {<br>transform: translate3d(0, 0, 20px);<br>}<br>50% {<br>transform: translate3d(8px, -14px, 35px);<br>}<br>}<br>@media (max-width: 768px) {<br>body {<br>background-attachment: scroll;<br>}<br>.hero-visual .hero-card {<br>transform: none;<br>animation-duration: 8s;<br>}<br>.hero::before,<br>.hero::after {<br>width: 180px;<br>filter: blur(50px);<br>}<br>}<br>@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {<br>*,<br>*::before,<br>*::after {<br>scroll-behavior: auto !important;<br>animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;<br>animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;<br>transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;<br>}<br>}<br>```<br>### 3\. Biar animasi hero benar-benar terlihat 3D<br>CSS di atas menyediakan efeknya, tetapi ilustrasi hero harus memiliki struktur yang sesuai. Pada HTML, cari bagian ilustrasi di samping teks halaman awal. Kalau elemen ilustrasi itu belum memiliki class, kamu bisa menambahkan class `hero-visual` pada pembungkusnya dan `hero-card` pada kartu utamanya.<br>Contoh struktur ilustrasi:<br>```html<br><div class="hero-visual"><br><div class="orb" aria-hidden="true"></div><br><div class="orb" aria-hidden="true"></div><br><div class="orb" aria-hidden="true"></div><br><div class="hero-card"><br><p>A MOMENT FOR YOU</p><br><div class="hero-heart" aria-hidden="true">♡</div><br><h3>Get to know yourself.</h3><br><p>Your feelings matter. Take it at your own pace.</p><br></div><br></div><br>```<br>Contoh ini hanya untuk area ilustrasi, bukan menggantikan seluruh `index.html`. Kalau HTML kamu sudah memiliki ilustrasi yang bagus, cukup tambahkan class yang sesuai pada elemen yang sudah ada.<br>### 4\. Tes ulang<br>Setelah menyimpan kedua file:<br>* Muat ulang website dengan `Ctrl + F5` agar CSS dan JavaScript terbaru dimuat.<br>* Geser intensitas ke angka 5. Angka `5/5` harus langsung muncul.<br>* Simpan mood. Hasilnya harus menampilkan `Intensitas 5/5`.<br>* Periksa tampilan awal: latar navy, kartu gelap, gradasi ungu-biru-cyan, dan elemen ilustrasi yang bergerak.<br>Satu hal lagi: screenshot lo memperlihatkan navbar dan bagian Mood Check-in yang sudah punya struktur sendiri. Supaya perubahan visualnya bisa benar-benar presisi—tanpa merusak layout yang sekarang—kirim isi `index.html` dan `style.css` yang sedang lo pakai. Gue bisa sesuaikan langsung dengan class asli Moodora, termasuk membuat ilustrasi 3D yang lebih interaktif mengikuti gerakan mouse, bukan hanya animasi mengambang. | tampilan pada perbaikan ini ternyata masih tetap dan hanya pada beranda yang berubah warna, oleh karena itu saya meminta untuk perbaikan seluruh page section untuk menyesuaikan warna nya menjadi gelap | Chatgpt |
| 2 | aduh ini malah beranda nya doang yang keganti dan font sama background nya nyatu karna sama sama gelap, seperti nya lebih bagus kalau background nya full gelap terus floating element dan section lainnya itu putih deh | Nahh, setuju banget. Menurut gue konsep yang lebih cocok buat Moodora itu dark background + white floating cards. Jadi nuansa gelapnya tetap dapet, tetapi tulisan dan fitur di setiap section jauh lebih jelas. Yang kemarin juga terlalu banyak selector umum, jadi beberapa elemen beranda bisa berubah tanpa seluruh section mengikuti desainnya.<br>Kita rapikan pakai konsep ini:<br>### Moodora<br>DARK MODE<br>### Your feelings, your space.<br>Kenali perasaanmu, pelan-pelan.<br>### Bagaimana perasaanmu hari ini?<br>Tidak ada jawaban yang salah.<br>### 😊<br>Senang<br>### 😌<br>Tenang<br>### 😔<br>Sedih<br>Calm Corner<br>Ambil jeda<br>Daily Journal<br>Ceritakan harimu<br>Pratinjau konsep warna — bukan screenshot langsung dari website lo.<br>### 1\. Ganti CSS lama dengan tema yang lebih konsisten<br>Supaya aturan sebelumnya tidak saling bertabrakan, hapus blok CSS tambahan `DARK DREAMY THEME` yang kemarin ditempel di bagian bawah, lalu ganti dengan CSS ini. Jangan hapus seluruh CSS layout lama lo; ini hanya pengganti blok tema tambahan.<br>```css<br>/* =========================================<br>MOODORA — DARK BACKGROUND + WHITE CARDS<br>Ganti blok tema tambahan sebelumnya dengan ini<br>========================================= */<br>:root {<br>--page-bg: #0b1020;<br>--page-bg-soft: #111a30;<br>--card-bg: #ffffff;<br>--card-bg-soft: #f4f5ff;<br>--text-light: #f5f7ff;<br>--text-dark: #252a42;<br>--text-muted-dark: #737b93;<br>--text-muted-light: #b9c4df;<br>--accent-blue: #819bff;<br>--accent-purple: #a992f5;<br>--accent-cyan: #78dce6;<br>--card-border: #e7e9f4;<br>--dark-border: rgba(174, 190, 255, 0.15);<br>--card-radius: 22px;<br>--card-shadow: 0 18px 48px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);<br>}<br>html {<br>scroll-behavior: smooth;<br>scroll-padding-top: 100px;<br>}<br>body {<br>color: var(--text-light);<br>background:<br>radial-gradient(ellipse at 12% 8%, rgba(93, 106, 207, 0.17), transparent 35%),<br>radial-gradient(ellipse at 88% 40%, rgba(139, 80, 189, 0.13), transparent 32%),<br>var(--page-bg);<br>background-attachment: fixed;<br>}<br>/* Navbar */<br>header,<br>.site-header,<br>.navbar {<br>color: var(--text-light);<br>background: rgba(11, 16, 32, 0.9);<br>border-color: var(--dark-border);<br>backdrop-filter: blur(18px);<br>-webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(18px);<br>}<br>nav a,<br>.nav-link {<br>color: #c5cee7;<br>}<br>nav a:hover,<br>.nav-link:hover,<br>.nav-link.active {<br>color: #ffffff;<br>}<br>/* Semua section memakai latar halaman gelap */<br>section {<br>background-color: transparent;<br>}<br>/* Card putih: komponen utama yang mengambang */<br>.card,<br>.feature-card,<br>.mood-card,<br>.journal-entry,<br>.question-card,<br>.habit-card,<br>.stat-card,<br>.reflection-card,<br>.calm-card {<br>color: var(--text-dark);<br>background: var(--card-bg);<br>border: 1px solid var(--card-border);<br>border-radius: var(--card-radius);<br>box-shadow: var(--card-shadow);<br>}<br>/* Teks di dalam kartu putih */<br>.card h1,<br>.card h2,<br>.card h3,<br>.card h4,<br>.card strong,<br>.card legend,<br>.feature-card h1,<br>.feature-card h2,<br>.feature-card h3,<br>.feature-card strong,<br>.mood-card h3,<br>.question-card legend,<br>.journal-entry strong,<br>.habit-card strong {<br>color: var(--text-dark);<br>}<br>.card p,<br>.card label,<br>.card small,<br>.feature-card p,<br>.journal-entry p,<br>.question-card p {<br>color: var(--text-muted-dark);<br>}<br>/* Efek hover pada kartu */<br>.card,<br>.feature-card,<br>.mood-card,<br>.journal-entry,<br>.habit-card {<br>transition:<br>transform 0.25s ease,<br>box-shadow 0.25s ease,<br>border-color 0.25s ease;<br>}<br>.feature-card:hover,<br>.mood-card:hover,<br>.journal-entry:hover {<br>transform: translateY(-4px);<br>border-color: #c7ceff;<br>box-shadow: 0 24px 55px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);<br>}<br>/* Mood pilihan */<br>[data-mood] {<br>color: var(--text-dark);<br>background: #ffffff;<br>border: 1px solid var(--card-border);<br>border-radius: 18px;<br>cursor: pointer;<br>}<br>[data-mood]:hover,<br>[data-mood].selected,<br>[data-mood].is-selected {<br>color: #34355d;<br>background: linear-gradient(145deg, #eef0ff, #f4eefe);<br>border-color: #9b9af5;<br>box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(116, 105, 205, 0.13);<br>}<br>/* Input pada kartu putih */<br>.card input:not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]):not([type="range"]),<br>.card textarea,<br>.card select,<br>.mood-form input:not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]):not([type="range"]),<br>.mood-form textarea,<br>.mood-form select {<br>color: var(--text-dark);<br>background: #f8f9ff;<br>border: 1px solid #dfe3f1;<br>border-radius: 12px;<br>}<br>input[type="range"] {<br>accent-color: #8179e9;<br>cursor: pointer;<br>}<br>input[type="checkbox"],<br>input[type="radio"] {<br>accent-color: #8179e9;<br>}<br>/* Nilai intensitas */<br>.intensity-value {<br>display: inline-flex;<br>align-items: center;<br>margin-top: 8px;<br>padding: 6px 12px;<br>color: #5046a2;<br>background: #efedff;<br>border-radius: 999px;<br>font-weight: 700;<br>}<br>/* Tombol utama */<br>button,<br>.btn-primary,<br>.nav-cta {<br>transition:<br>transform 0.2s ease,<br>box-shadow 0.2s ease;<br>}<br>button:hover,<br>.btn-primary:hover,<br>.nav-cta:hover {<br>transform: translateY(-2px);<br>}<br>button:focus-visible,<br>a:focus-visible,<br>input:focus-visible,<br>textarea:focus-visible {<br>outline: 3px solid var(--accent-cyan);<br>outline-offset: 3px;<br>}<br>/* Hero tetap gelap, berbeda dari card putih */<br>.hero {<br>position: relative;<br>isolation: isolate;<br>overflow: hidden;<br>color: var(--text-light);<br>background:<br>radial-gradient(ellipse at 80% 35%, rgba(121, 100, 225, 0.2), transparent 34%),<br>radial-gradient(ellipse at 18% 80%, rgba(72, 192, 210, 0.12), transparent 32%);<br>}<br>.hero h1,<br>.hero h2,<br>.hero h3 {<br>color: #ffffff;<br>}<br>.hero p {<br>color: var(--text-muted-light);<br>}<br>.hero h1 span,<br>.hero .gradient-text {<br>color: transparent;<br>background: linear-gradient(100deg, #9bb5ff, #c6a9ff, #81e4e8);<br>background-clip: text;<br>-webkit-background-clip: text;<br>}<br>/* Ilustrasi hero */<br>.hero-visual {<br>position: relative;<br>perspective: 1000px;<br>transform-style: preserve-3d;<br>}<br>.hero-visual .hero-card {<br>position: relative;<br>color: var(--text-dark);<br>background: linear-gradient(145deg, #ffffff, #f0f2ff);<br>border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.65);<br>border-radius: 28px;<br>box-shadow:<br>0 35px 80px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3),<br>0 0 45px rgba(133, 139, 255, 0.13);<br>transform-style: preserve-3d;<br>animation: moodoraCardFloat 6s ease-in-out infinite;<br>}<br>.hero-visual .hero-card h3,<br>.hero-visual .hero-card p {<br>color: var(--text-dark);<br>}<br>.hero-visual .orb {<br>position: absolute;<br>width: 72px;<br>aspect-ratio: 1;<br>border-radius: 50%;<br>background: radial-gradient(<br>circle at 30% 25%,<br>#f0f1ff,<br>#a8a2ff 35%,<br>#655ac4 75%,<br>#33315e<br>);<br>box-shadow:<br>inset -8px -10px 18px rgba(23, 24, 57, 0.35),<br>0 0 35px rgba(142, 145, 255, 0.35);<br>animation: moodoraOrbFloat 5s ease-in-out infinite;<br>pointer-events: none;<br>}<br>.hero-visual .orb:nth-child(2) {<br>width: 38px;<br>animation-delay: -2s;<br>}<br>.hero-visual .orb:nth-child(3) {<br>width: 22px;<br>animation-delay: -3.5s;<br>}<br>/* Progress bar */<br>#question-progress {<br>background: #e6e9f7;<br>border-radius: 999px;<br>overflow: hidden;<br>}<br>#question-progress-fill {<br>background: linear-gradient(90deg, #819bff, #a992f5, #78dce6);<br>border-radius: 999px;<br>transition: width 0.35s ease;<br>}<br>/* Hasil mood */<br>#mood-result,<br>#reflection-result {<br>color: var(--text-dark);<br>background: #ffffff;<br>border: 1px solid var(--card-border);<br>border-radius: var(--card-radius);<br>box-shadow: var(--card-shadow);<br>padding: 22px;<br>}<br>#mood-result p,<br>#reflection-result p {<br>color: var(--text-muted-dark);<br>}<br>/* Pesan keselamatan tetap terlihat jelas */<br>#safety-support-message {<br>color: #3c2631;<br>background: #fff3f0;<br>border: 1px solid #f2c8bf;<br>border-radius: 18px;<br>padding: 20px;<br>}<br>#safety-support-message h3,<br>#safety-support-message p {<br>color: #3c2631;<br>}<br>/* Reveal */<br>.reveal-on-scroll {<br>opacity: 0;<br>transform: translateY(18px);<br>transition: opacity 0.6s ease, transform 0.6s ease;<br>}<br>.reveal-on-scroll.is-visible {<br>opacity: 1;<br>transform: translateY(0);<br>}<br>@keyframes moodoraCardFloat {<br>0%, 100% {<br>transform: rotateY(-6deg) rotateX(3deg) translateY(0);<br>}<br>50% {<br>transform: rotateY(-2deg) rotateX(1deg) translateY(-8px);<br>}<br>}<br>@keyframes moodoraOrbFloat {<br>0%, 100% {<br>transform: translate3d(0, 0, 20px);<br>}<br>50% {<br>transform: translate3d(8px, -14px, 35px);<br>}<br>}<br>@media (max-width: 768px) {<br>body {<br>background-attachment: scroll;<br>}<br>.hero-visual .hero-card {<br>transform: none;<br>animation-duration: 8s;<br>}<br>.hero-visual .orb {<br>width: 48px;<br>}<br>}<br>@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {<br>*,<br>*::before,<br>*::after {<br>scroll-behavior: auto !important;<br>animation: none !important;<br>transition: none !important;<br>}<br>.reveal-on-scroll {<br>opacity: 1;<br>transform: none;<br>}<br>}<br>```<br>### 2\. Kenapa kemarin hanya beranda yang berubah?<br>Karena CSS yang gue berikan sebelumnya terlalu mengandalkan class generik seperti `.card` dan `.feature-cIya, HTML yang kamu kirim secara struktur sudah cukup lengkap dan bisa dipakai sebagai dasar Moodora. Ada satu hal penting: beberapa fitur baru bisa benar-benar berfungsi setelah JavaScript-nya dibuat, tetapi itu tidak menghalangi kita memperbaiki CSS sekarang.<br>Kita bikin ulang `style.css` berdasarkan HTML terbarumu, supaya revisinya nggak saling bertabrakan.<br>Konsep desain yang kita pakai:<br>* Background utama: dark navy, bukan putih.<br>* Section dan kartu: putih atau putih kebiruan agar kontras.<br>* Warna aksen: ungu, biru, mint, dan sedikit pink.<br>* Hero: visual mengambang dengan efek kedalaman 3D.<br>* Interaksi: hover, animasi lembut, efek fokus, dan responsif untuk HP maupun laptop.<br>* Kompatibel dengan HTML kamu: menggunakan nama class dan ID yang sudah ada.<br>### CSS Moodora — versi revisi<br>Buka file `style.css` di GitHub, hapus isi CSS lama, lalu ganti dengan kode lengkap berikut.<br>```css<br>/* ========================================<br>MOODORA — DARK SPACE + LIGHT CARDS<br>======================================== */<br>:root {<br>--bg: #171b2e;<br>--bg-light: #202640;<br>--white: #ffffff;<br>--paper: #f5f6fc;<br>--text: #252a43;<br>--muted: #777d95;<br>--light-text: #f5f6ff;<br>--purple: #8175e8;<br>--blue: #789cf5;<br>--mint: #8bd6bd;<br>--pink: #f1b8d4;<br>--border: #e6e8f2;<br>--radius: 22px;<br>--shadow: 0 18px 45px #080b1d30;<br>}<br>* {<br>box-sizing: border-box;<br>margin: 0;<br>padding: 0;<br>}<br>html {<br>scroll-behavior: smooth;<br>scroll-padding-top: 95px;<br>}<br>body {<br>background: var(--bg);<br>color: var(--light-text);<br>font-family: "DM Sans", sans-serif;<br>line-height: 1.7;<br>overflow-x: hidden;<br>}<br>body, button, input, textarea, select {<br>font-family: "DM Sans", sans-serif;<br>}<br>h1, h2, h3, h4, .brand-name {<br>font-family: "Plus Jakarta Sans", sans-serif;<br>line-height: 1.3;<br>}<br>h1, h2, h3, h4, p {<br>overflow-wrap: break-word;<br>}<br>a {<br>color: inherit;<br>text-decoration: none;<br>}<br>button, input, textarea, select {<br>font-size: inherit;<br>}<br>button, a, input, textarea, select {<br>-webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;<br>}<br>button {<br>cursor: pointer;<br>}<br>button:disabled {<br>opacity: .45;<br>cursor: not-allowed;<br>}<br>img, svg {<br>max-width: 100%;<br>}<br>[hidden] {<br>display: none !important;<br>}<br>.container {<br>width: min(1160px, 90%);<br>margin-inline: auto;<br>}<br>.section {<br>position: relative;<br>padding: 100px 0;<br>}<br>.section-title {<br>font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 4vw, 3rem);<br>letter-spacing: -1.2px;<br>margin: 12px 0 18px;<br>}<br>.section-description {<br>max-width: 650px;<br>color: #b9bfd5;<br>font-size: 1.02rem;<br>}<br>.section-heading {<br>margin-bottom: 38px;<br>}<br>.eyebrow {<br>display: inline-flex;<br>align-items: center;<br>gap: 9px;<br>color: var(--mint);<br>font-size: .76rem;<br>font-weight: 800;<br>letter-spacing: 2px;<br>}<br>.status-dot {<br>width: 8px;<br>height: 8px;<br>border-radius: 50%;<br>background: var(--mint);<br>box-shadow: 0 0 12px #8bd6bd90;<br>}<br>/* ========================================<br>NAVBAR<br>======================================== */<br>.site-header {<br>position: sticky;<br>top: 0;<br>z-index: 100;<br>background: #171b2eea;<br>backdrop-filter: blur(18px);<br>border-bottom: 1px solid #ffffff12;<br>}<br>.navbar {<br>min-height: 82px;<br>display: flex;<br>align-items: center;<br>justify-content: space-between;<br>gap: 25px;<br>}<br>.brand {<br>display: inline-flex;<br>align-items: center;<br>gap: 9px;<br>flex-shrink: 0;<br>}<br>.brand-icon {<br>width: 42px;<br>height: 42px;<br>color: var(--purple);<br>display: grid;<br>place-items: center;<br>}<br>.brand-name {<br>color: white;<br>font-size: 1.65rem;<br>font-weight: 800;<br>letter-spacing: -1px;<br>}<br>.brand-name span {<br>color: var(--mint);<br>}<br>.nav-menu {<br>display: flex;<br>align-items: center;<br>gap: 26px;<br>}<br>.nav-link {<br>color: #c6cbe0;<br>font-size: .88rem;<br>font-weight: 600;<br>transition: color .25s;<br>}<br>.nav-link:hover,<br>.nav-link.active {<br>color: white;<br>}<br>.nav-link.active {<br>box-shadow: 0 2px var(--mint);<br>padding-bottom: 8px;<br>}<br>.nav-cta {<br>background: white;<br>color: var(--text);<br>padding: 12px 17px;<br>border-radius: 13px;<br>font-size: .85rem;<br>font-weight: 800;<br>transition: .25s;<br>}<br>.nav-cta:hover {<br>background: var(--mint);<br>transform: translateY(-2px);<br>}<br>.menu-toggle {<br>display: none;<br>border: 0;<br>background: transparent;<br>padding: 8px;<br>}<br>.menu-toggle span {<br>display: block;<br>width: 25px;<br>height: 2px;<br>margin: 5px;<br>background: white;<br>border-radius: 5px;<br>}<br>/* ========================================<br>BUTTONS<br>======================================== */<br>.button {<br>display: inline-flex;<br>align-items: center;<br>justify-content: center;<br>gap: 10px;<br>min-height: 48px;<br>padding: 13px 20px;<br>border: 1px solid transparent;<br>border-radius: 13px;<br>font-weight: 700;<br>font-size: .9rem;<br>transition: transform .25s, box-shadow .25s,<br>background .25s, color .25s;<br>}<br>.button:hover:not(:disabled) {<br>transform: translateY(-3px);<br>}<br>.button-primary {<br>color: white;<br>background: var(--purple);<br>box-shadow: 0 8px 22px #8175e83a;<br>}<br>.button-primary:hover {<br>background: #7164df;<br>box-shadow: 0 12px 28px #8175e855;<br>}<br>.button-secondary {<br>color: var(--text);<br>background: white;<br>border-color: var(--border);<br>}<br>.button-secondary:hover {<br>background: #edf0ff;<br>}<br>.button-danger {<br>background: #fff0f1;<br>color: #b93852;<br>border-color: #ffd4da;<br>}<br>.button-group {<br>display: flex;<br>align-items: center;<br>flex-wrap: wrap;<br>gap: 12px;<br>margin-top: 22px;<br>}<br>.text-link {<br>display: inline-flex;<br>align-items: center;<br>gap: 8px;<br>color: #6559cc;<br>font-weight: 800;<br>font-size: .9rem;<br>transition: gap .2s;<br>}<br>.text-link:hover {<br>gap: 14px;<br>}<br>/* ========================================<br>HERO<br>======================================== */<br>.hero {<br>min-height: 660px;<br>display: flex;<br>align-items: center;<br>isolation: isolate;<br>overflow: hidden;<br>background:<br>radial-gradient(ellipse at 80% 45%, #4c4b8730, transparent 38%),<br>radial-gradient(ellipse at 10% 90%, #507fa01c, transparent 38%);<br>}<br>.hero-grid {<br>display: grid;<br>grid-template-columns: 1.1fr .9fr;<br>align-items: center;<br>gap: 50px;<br>}<br>.hero-content {<br>position: relative;<br>z-index: 2;<br>}<br>.hero h1 {<br>max-width: 720px;<br>margin: 25px 0;<br>font-size: clamp(2.6rem, 5.5vw, 4.7rem);<br>letter-spacing: -2.8px;<br>line-height: 1.13;<br>}<br>.hero h1 span {<br>color: var(--mint);<br>}<br>.hero-description {<br>max-width: 570px;<br>color: #c1c7dd;<br>font-size: 1.05rem;<br>}<br>.hero-actions {<br>display: flex;<br>flex-wrap: wrap;<br>gap: 13px;<br>margin-top: 30px;<br>}<br>.hero-note {<br>margin-top: 24px;<br>color: #aeb5cc;<br>font-size: .86rem;<br>}<br>.hero-decoration {<br>position: absolute;<br>z-index: -1;<br>pointer-events: none;<br>}<br>.hero-orb {<br>width: 300px;<br>height: 300px;<br>top: 10%;<br>right: -100px;<br>border-radius: 50%;<br>background: linear-gradient(135deg, #8175e845, #8bd6bd12);<br>filter: blur(2px);<br>}<br>.hero-orb-small {<br>width: 100px;<br>height: 100px;<br>bottom: 8%;<br>left: 35%;<br>border-radius: 50%;<br>background: #8bd6bd12;<br>}<br>.hero-star {<br>top: 15%;<br>left: 6%;<br>color: var(--mint);<br>font-size: 2rem;<br>animation: starFloat 5s ease-in-out infinite;<br>}<br>.hero-visual {<br>position: relative;<br>min-height: 440px;<br>display: grid;<br>place-items: center;<br>perspective: 1000px;<br>}<br>.visual-card {<br>position: relative;<br>z-index: 2;<br>width: min(360px, 88%);<br>padding: 32px 24px;<br>text-align: center;<br>color: var(--text);<br>background: linear-gradient(145deg, white, #eef0ff);<br>border: 1px solid #ffffffb0;<br>border-radius: 30px;<br>box-shadow: 0 35px 80px #080b1d55,<br>inset 0 1px white;<br>transform: rotateY(-9deg) rotateX(4deg);<br>animation: cardFloat 6s ease-in-out infinite;<br>}<br>.visual-card-label {<br>color: #777d95;<br>font-size: .68rem;<br>font-weight: 800;<br>letter-spacing: 2px;<br>}<br>.visual-symbol {<br>width: 150px;<br>margin: 22px auto;<br>color: var(--purple);<br>}<br>.visual-card h2 {<br>font-size: 1.25rem;<br>}<br>.visual-card p {<br>margin-top: 8px;<br>color: var(--muted);<br>font-size: .8rem;<br>}<br>.orbit {<br>position: absolute;<br>width: 390px;<br>height: 390px;<br>border: 1px dashed #a8b0ff55;<br>border-radius: 50%;<br>animation: orbitSpin 30s linear infinite;<br>}<br>.orbit-two {<br>width: 450px;<br>height: 450px;<br>border-color: #8bd6bd35;<br>animation-direction: reverse;<br>}<br>.floating-note {<br>position: absolute;<br>z-index: 3;<br>display: flex;<br>align-items: center;<br>gap: 10px;<br>padding: 14px 17px;<br>color: var(--text);<br>background: white;<br>border-radius: 15px;<br>box-shadow: var(--shadow);<br>font-size: .8rem;<br>font-weight: 800;<br>animation: noteFloat 5s ease-in-out infinite;<br>}<br>.floating-note span {<br>color: var(--purple);<br>font-size: 1.3rem;<br>}<br>.floating-note-top {<br>top: 45px;<br>right: -8px;<br>}<br>.floating-note-bottom {<br>bottom: 35px;<br>left: -5px;<br>animation-delay: -2s;<br>}<br>.floating-note-bottom .status-dot {<br>background: var(--mint);<br>}<br>/* ========================================<br>INTRO<br>======================================== */<br>.intro-section {<br>background: #202640;<br>}<br>.intro-grid {<br>display: grid;<br>grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;<br>gap: 65px;<br>align-items: center;<br>}<br>.intro-copy {<br>color: #c1c7dd;<br>}<br>.intro-copy p + p {<br>margin-top: 15px;<br>}<br>/* ========================================<br>FEATURE CARDS<br>======================================== */<br>.explore-section {<br>background: var(--bg);<br>}<br>.feature-grid {<br>display: grid;<br>grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));<br>gap: 20px;<br>}<br>.feature-card {<br>position: relative;<br>overflow: hidden;<br>display: flex;<br>flex-direction: column;<br>align-items: flex-start;<br>min-height: 300px;<br>padding: 27px;<br>color: var(--text);<br>background: white;<br>border: 1px solid #ffffff70;<br>border-radius: var(--radius);<br>box-shadow: 0 8px 25px #080b1d15;<br>transition: transform .3s, box-shadow .3s;<br>}<br>.feature-card::after {<br>content: "";<br>position: absolute;<br>width: 120px;<br>height: 120px;<br>right: -45px;<br>top: -45px;<br>border-radius: 50%;<br>background: #8175e817;<br>transition: transform .4s;<br>}<br>.feature-card:hover {<br>transform: translateY(-8px);<br>box-shadow: var(--shadow);<br>}<br>.feature-card:hover::after {<br>transform: scale(1.7);<br>}<br>.feature-lavender {<br>background: linear-gradient(145deg, white, #f0edff);<br>}<br>.feature-mint {<br>background: linear-gradient(145deg, white, #eafaf4);<br>}<br>.feature-blue {<br>background: linear-gradient(145deg, white, #edf3ff);<br>}<br>.feature-neutral {<br>background: linear-gradient(145deg, white, #f3f3f8);<br>}<br>.feature-icon {<br>display: grid;<br>place-items: center;<br>width: 52px;<br>height: 52px;<br>margin-bottom: 20px;<br>border-radius: 16px;<br>color: #665bd0;<br>background: #8175e81c;<br>font-size: 1.7rem;<br>}<br>.feature-number {<br>color: #858ba3;<br>font-size: .68rem;<br>font-weight: 800;<br>letter-spacing: 1.5px;<br>}<br>.feature-card h3 {<br>margin: 9px 0;<br>font-size: 1.2rem;<br>}<br>.feature-card p {<br>flex: 1;<br>margin-bottom: 20px;<br>color: #737991;<br>font-size: .9rem;<br>}<br>/* ========================================<br>FORM SECTIONS<br>======================================== */<br>.checkin-section,<br>.garden-section,<br>constellation-section,<br>journal-section,<br>future-letter-section,<br>.privacy-section {<br>background: var(--bg-light);<br>}<br>.reflection-section,<br>.calm-section,<br>.inner-world-section,<br>.small-steps-section {<br>background: var(--bg);<br>}<br>.panel {<br>color: var(--text);<br>background: var(--white);<br>border: 1px solid #ffffff80;<br>border-radius: var(--radius);<br>padding: clamp(22px, 4vw, 38px);<br>box-shadow: var(--shadow);<br>}<br>.form-group {<br>min-width: 0;<br>margin-bottom: 28px;<br>}<br>.form-group > label,<br>.form-group legend {<br>display: block;<br>margin-bottom: 9px;<br>color: var(--text);<br>font-weight: {<br>box-shadow: none;<br>color: var(--mint);<br>}<br>.nav-cta {<br>margin-top: 10px;<br>text-align: center;<br>}<br>.hero {<br>min-height: auto;<br>padding: 70px 0 55px;<br>}<br>.hero-grid,<br>.intro-grid,<br>.calm-grid,<br>.journal-grid {<br>grid-template-columns: 1fr;<br>}<br>.hero-grid {<br>gap: 20px;<br>}<br>.hero h1 {<br>font-size: clamp(2.4rem, 10vw, 3.7rem);<br>letter-spacing: -1.8px;<br>}<br>.hero-visual {<br>min-height: 390px;<br>}<br>.visual-card {<br>width: min(310px, 82%);<br>padding: 25px 18px;<br>}<br>.orbit {<br>width: 310px;<br>height: 310px;<br>}<br>.orbit-two {<br>width: 350px;<br>height: 350px;<br>}<br>.floating-note {<br>padding: 11px 12px;<br>font-size: .72rem;<br>}<br>.floating-note-top {<br>top: 25px;<br>right: 0;<br>}<br>.floating-note-bottom {<br>bottom: 15px;<br>left: 0;<br>}<br>.intro-grid {<br>gap: 20px;<br>}<br>.feature-grid,<br>.support-grid {<br>grid-template-columns: 1fr;<br>}<br>.feature-card {<br>min-height: 250px;<br>}<br>.mood-options {<br>grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));<br>}<br>.factor-options {<br>grid-template-columns: 1fr;<br>}<br>.panel {<br>padding: 22px 18px;<br>}<br>.questionnaire-info {<br>flex-direction: column;<br>align-items: center;<br>gap: 7px;<br>}<br>.question-navigation .button {<br>flex: 1;<br>}<br>.garden-visual {<br>height: 230px;<br>}<br>.garden-progress {<br>align-items: flex-start;<br>}<br>.breathing-visual {<br>width: 190px;<br>height: 190px;<br>}<br>.support-panel {<br>padding: 28px 22px;<br>}<br>.footer-main {<br>grid-template-columns: 1fr;<br>gap: 25px;<br>}<br>.footer-links {<br>grid-column: auto;<br>display: grid;<br>}<br>.footer-bottom {<br>flex-direction: column;<br>}<br>.button-group .button {<br>flex: 1 1 auto;<br>}<br>.toast-container {<br>right: 12px;<br>bottom: 12px;<br>left: 12px;<br>}<br>.toast {<br>max-width: none;<br>}<br>}<br>/* ========================================<br>AKSESIBILITAS<br>======================================== */<br>:focus-visible {<br>outline: 3px solid var(--mint);<br>outline-offset: 4px;<br>}<br>@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {<br>*,<br>*::before,<br>*::after {<br>scroll-behavior: auto !important;<br>animation-duration: .01ms !important;<br>animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;<br>transition-duration: .01ms !important;<br>}<br>}<br>```<br>### Cara memasangnya di GitHub<br>* Buka repository Moodora kamu.<br>* Buka file `style.css`, lalu tekan ikon pensil untuk mengedit.<br>* Hapus CSS versi lama dan tempel kode di atas.<br>* Tekan Commit changes.<br>* Buka website GitHub Pages dan refresh. Kalau tampilannya belum berubah, coba refresh paksa dengan `Ctrl + F5` di laptop.<br>Catatan penting: CSS ini mengubah tampilan semua section yang ada di HTML kamu, bukan hanya beranda. Namun, CSS hanya mengatur tampilan—intensitas mood yang selalu menunjukkan `3 / 5`, kuesioner, penyimpanan journal, dan interaksi lainnya tetap membutuhkan JavaScript yang sesuai.<br>Setelah CSS ini terpasang, kita bisa lanjut merapikan `script.js` agar cocok dengan ID di HTML terbaru kamu, sehingga tidak ada fitur yang rusak akibat revisi sebelumnya.<br>ard`, sedangkan belum tentu seluruh bagian HTML Moodora memakai class tersebut. Beberapa section juga mungkin punya warna latar sendiri yang menimpa warna `body`.<br>CSS baru ini membuat aturan yang lebih terarah: latar halaman gelap, hero gelap, dan kartu-kartu berwarna putih. Namun, komponen yang memakai class berbeda dari daftar di atas tetap perlu disesuaikan dengan HTML aslinya.<br>Saran gue: jangan tempel CSS tambahan baru berkali-kali lagi. Kalau setelah mengganti blok tema ini masih ada section yang gelap dengan tulisan gelap, kirim `index.html` dan `style.css` terbaru. Gue akan cocokkan nama class-nya supaya seluruh Moodora konsisten dari beranda sampai Journal, Calm Corner, dan Refleksi Diri. | setelah ini nya harus diperbaiki agar fitur fitur bisa tersambung kembali | Chatgpt |

checkin-section" id="check-in"><br><div class="container"><br><div class="section-heading"><br><span class="eyebrow">01 / MOOD CHECK-IN</span><br><h2 class="section-title">Apa yang kamu rasakan hari mood-form"><br><fieldset class="form-group"><br><legend>1. Pilih mood kamu</legend><br><p class="field-hint"><br>Pilih satu yang paling sesuai dengan keadaanmu sekarang.<br></p><br><div class="mood-options"><br><label class="mood-option"><br><input type="radio" name="mood" value="senang" required><br><span class="mood-emoji" aria-hidden="true">☀</span><br><span>Senang</span><br></label><br><label class="mood-option"><br><input type="radio" name="mood" value="tenang"><br><span class="mood-emoji" aria-hidden="true">◌</span><br><span>Tenang</span><br></label><br><label class="mood-option"><br><input type="radio" name="mood" value="biasa"><br><span class="mood-emoji" aria-hidden="true">〰</span><br><span>Biasa saja</span><br></label><br><label class="mood-option"><br><input type="radio" name="mood" value="cemas"><br><span class="mood-emoji" aria-hidden="true">∿</span><br><span>Cemas</span><br></label><br><label class="mood-option"><br><input type="radio" name="mood" value="sedih"><br><span class="mood-emoji" aria-hidden="true">☁</span><br><span>Sedih</span><br></label><br><label class="mood-option"><br><input type="radio" name="mood" value="lelah"><br><span class="mood-emoji" aria-hidden="true">⌁</span><br><span>Lelah</span><br></label><br></div><br></fieldset><br><div class="form-group"><br><label for="mood-intensity"><br>2. Seberapa kuat perasaan ini?<br></label><br><p class="field-hint"><br>Geser dari 1 (ringan) sampai 5 (sangat kuat).<br></p><br><div class="range-header"><br><span>Intensitas</span><br><output id="intensity-value" for="mood-intensity">3 / 5</output><br></div><br><input<br>type="range"<br>id="mood-intensity"<br>name="intensity"<br>min="1"<br>max="5"<br>value="3"<br>><br><div class="range-labels"><br><span>Ringan</span><br><span>Sangat kuat</span><br></div><br></div><br><fieldset class="form-group"><br><legend>3. Apa yang mungkin memengaruhi mood kamu?</legend><br><p class="field-hint"><br>Kamu boleh memilih lebih dari satu.<br></p><br><div class="factor-options"><br><label><input type="checkbox" name="factors" value="sekolah"> Sekolah atau tugas</label><br><label><input type="checkbox" name="factors" value="keluarga"> Keluarga</label><br><label><input type="checkbox" name="factors" value="teman"> Pertemanan</label><br><label><input type="checkbox" name="factors" value="istirahat"> Tidur atau istirahat</label><br><label><input type="checkbox" name="factors" value="diri"> Pikiran tentang diri sendiri</label><br><label><input type="checkbox" name="factors" value="lainnya"> Hal lainnya</label><br></div><br></fieldset><br><div class="form-group"><br><label for="mood-note"><br>4. Ada hal yang ingin kamu catat? (opsional)<br></label><br><textarea<br>id="mood-note"<br>name="note"<br>rows="3"<br>maxlength="1000"<br>placeholder="Tulis seperlunya. Kamu tidak harus menceritakan semuanya..."<br>></textarea><br></div><br><button class="button button-primary" type="submit"><br>Simpan Mood <span aria-hidden="true">↗</span><br></button><br><p id="mood-result" class="form-feedback" aria-live="polite"></p><br></form><br></div><br></section><br><!-- =========================<br>KUESIONER / REFLEKSI DIRI<br>========================== --><br><section class="section reflection-section" id="reflection"><br><div class="container"><br><div class="section-heading"><br><span class="eyebrow">02 / SELF-REFLECTION</span><br><h2 class="section-title"><br>Yuk, kenali apa yang sedang kamu rasakan.<br></h2><br><p class="section-description"><br>Jawab 10 pertanyaan tentang pengalamanmu selama dua minggu<br>terakhir. Hasilnya berupa bahan refleksi, bukan diagnosis.<br></p><br></div><br><div class="panel questionnaire-panel"><br><div class="questionnaire-intro" id="questionnaire-intro"><br><div class="questionnaire-symbol" aria-hidden="true">✳</div><br><h3>Luangkan waktu untuk dirimu sendiri.</h3><br><p><br>Tidak ada jawaban yang membuatmu lebih baik atau lebih buruk.<br>Jawablah berdasarkan apa yang benar-benar kamu alami.<br></p><br><ul class="questionnaire-info"><br><li>10 pertanyaan</li><br><li>Sekitar 3–5 menit</li><br><li>Untuk refleksi pribadi</li><br></ul><br><button<br>type="button"<br>class="button button-primary"<br>id="start-questionnaire"<br>><br>Mulai Refleksi <span aria-hidden="true">↗</span><br></button><br></div><br><form id="questionnaire-form" hidden><br><div class="progress-header"><br><span id="question-counter">Pertanyaan 1 dari 10</span><br><span id="question-percentage">10%</span><br></div><br><div<br>class="progress-track"<br>role="progressbar"<br>id="question-progress"<br>aria-label="Progres kuesioner"<br>aria-valuemin="0"<br>aria-valuemax="10"<br>aria-valuenow="1"<br>><br><div class="progress-fill" id="question-progress-fill"></div><br></div><br><p class="field-hint"><br>Selama dua minggu terakhir, seberapa sering kamu mengalami hal berikut?<br></p><br><!-- Pertanyaan ditampilkan satu per satu oleh JavaScript --><br><div id="question-container" aria-live="polite"></div><br><div button-primary"<br>id="question-next"<br>><br>Selanjutnya <span aria-hidden="true">→</span><br></button><br><button<br>type="submit"<br>class="button button-primary"<br>id="question-submit"<br>hidden<br>><br>Lihat Refleksiku<br></button><br></div><br><p id="question-error" class="form-feedback" aria-live="polite"></p><br></form><br><!-- Hasil refleksi --><br><div id="reflection-result" class="reflection-result" hidden><br><span class="eyebrow">YOUR REFLECTION</span><br><h3 id="reflection-result-title">Mari pahami perasaanmu.</h3><br><p id="reflection-result-description"></p><br><div class="reflection-advice"><br><h4>Langkah kecil yang bisa kamu coba</h4><br><ul id="reflection-advice-list"></ul><br></div><br><p class="disclaimer"><br>Hasil ini bukan diagnosis dan tidak dapat menentukan<br>kondisi kesehatan mental seseorang. bahagia, tetapi tentang tetap memberi<br>perhatian kepada dirimu sendiri.<br></p><br></div><br><div class="garden-panel panel"><br><div class="garden-header"><br><div><br><span class="eyebrow">YOUR MOOD GARDEN</span><br><h3>Taman kecilmu</h3><br></div><br><span class="garden-status" id="garden-status"><br>Siap bertumbuh<br></span><br></div><br><!-- Dekorasi taman akan dikembangkan dengan CSS dan id="calm-corner"><br><div class="container"><br><div class="section-heading"><br><span class="eyebrow">05 / TAKE A BREATH</span><br><h2 class="section-title">Kamu boleh berhenti sebentar.</h2><br><p elemen di HTML tersebut. Kita juga pertahankan desain gelap dengan kartu terang—JavaScript ini mengurus interaksi, sedangkan warna dan tampilan tetap diatur oleh CSS.<br>Cara memasangnya di GitHub:<br>* Buka repositori Moodora → file `script.js`.<br>* Tekan ikon pensil untuk mengedit.<br>* Hapus kode lama, lalu tempel kode di bawah.<br>* Simpan dengan Commit changes.<br>### Kode lengkap `script.js`<br>```javascript<br>"use strict";<br>document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {<br>// ========================================<br>// MOODORA - JAVASCRIPT UTAMA<br>// ========================================<br>const $ = (selector, root = document) =><br>root.querySelector(selector);<br>const $$ = (selector, root = document) =><br>[...root.querySelectorAll(selector)];<br>const KEYS = {<br>moods: "moodora_moods_v1",<br>journal: "moodora_journal_v1",<br>garden: "moodora_garden_v1",<br>letters: "moodora_letters_v1",<br>steps: "moodora_steps_v1"<br>};<br>function load(key, fallback) {<br>try {<br>const data = localStorage.getItem(key);<br>return data ? JSON.parse(data) : fallback;<br>} catch {<br>return fallback;<br>}<br>}<br>function save(key, value) {<br>try {<br>localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));<br>return true;<br>} catch {<br>toast("Penyimpanan browser tidak tersedia.");<br>return false;<br>}<br>}<br>let moods = load(KEYS.moods, []);<br>let journals = load(KEYS.journal, []);<br>let gardenCount = Number(load(KEYS.garden, 0)) \|\| 0;<br>let letters = load(KEYS.letters, []);<br>let savedSteps = load(KEYS.steps, []);<br>if (!Array.isArray(moods)) moods = [];<br>if (!Array.isArray(journals)) journals = [];<br>if (!Array.isArray(letters)) letters = [];<br>if (!Array.isArray(savedSteps)) savedSteps = [];<br>const moodNames = {<br>senang: "Senang",<br>tenang: "Tenang",<br>biasa: "Biasa saja",<br>cemas: "Cemas",<br>sedih: "Sedih",<br>lelah: "Lelah"<br>};<br>function escapeHTML(value) {<br>return String(value ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, char => ({<br>"&": "&amp;",<br>"<": "&lt;",<br>">": "&gt;",<br>'"': "&quot;",<br>"'": "&#39;"<br>})[char]);<br>}<br>function showFeedback(id, message, error = false) {<br>const element = $("#" + id);<br>if (!element) return;<br>element.textContent = message;<br>element.classList.toggle("is-error", error);<br>element.classList.toggle("is-success", !error && !!message);<br>}<br>function toast(message) {<br>const container = $("#toast-container");<br>if (!container) {<br>console.info(message);<br>return;<br>}<br>const item = document.createElement("div");<br>item.className = "toast";<br>item.textContent = message;<br>item.setAttribute("role", "status");<br>container.appendChild(item);<br>requestAnimationFrame(() => item.classList.add("show"));<br>setTimeout(() => {<br>item.classList.remove("show");<br>setTimeout(() => item.remove(), 300);<br>}, 3000);<br>}<br>function formatDate(value, withTime = true) {<br>const date = new Date(value);<br>if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "Tanggal tidak diketahui";<br>return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("id-ID", {<br>day: "numeric",<br>month: "long",<br>year: "numeric",<br>...(withTime ? {<br>hour: "2-digit",<br>minute: "2-digit"<br>} : {})<br>}).format(date);<br>}<br>function today() {<br>const date = new Date();<br>const local = new Date(<br>date.getTime() - date.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000<br>);<br>return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);<br>}<br>// ========================================<br>// NAVIGASI DAN ANIMASI<br>// ========================================<br>const year = $("#current-year");<br>if (year) year.textContent = new Date().getFullYear();<br>const menuToggle = $("#menu-toggle");<br>const navMenu = $("#nav-menu");<br>if (menuToggle && navMenu) {<br>menuToggle.addEventListener("click", () => {<br>const opened = navMenu.classList.toggle("is-open");<br>menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(opened));<br>menuToggle.setAttribute(<br>"aria-label",<br>opened ? "Tutup navigasi" : "Buka navigasi"<br>);<br>});<br>$$(".nav-menu a").forEach(link => {<br>link.addEventListener("click", () => {<br>navMenu.classList.remove("is-open");<br>menuToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");<br>});<br>});<br>}<br>if ("IntersectionObserver" in window) {<br>const sections = $$(".nav-link")<br>.map(link => $(link.getAttribute("href")))<br>.filter(Boolean);<br>const navObserver = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {<br>entries.forEach(entry => {<br>if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;<br>$$(".nav-link").forEach(link => {<br>link.classList.toggle(<br>"active",<br>link.getAttribute("href") === "#" + entry.target.id<br>);<br>});<br>});<br>}, {<br>rootMargin: "-25% 0px -65% 0px"<br>});<br>sections.forEach(section => navObserver.observe(section));<br>if (!window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) {<br>const elements = $$(".feature-card, .panel, .support-card, .section-heading");<br>elements.forEach(element => element.classList.add("reveal-on-scroll"));<br>const revealObserver = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {<br>entries.forEach(entry => {<br>if (entry.isIntersecting) {<br>entry.target.classList.add("is-visible");<br>revealObserver.unobserve(entry.target);<br>}<br>});<br>}, { threshold: 0.12 });<br>elements.forEach(element => revealObserver.observe(element));<br>}<br>}<br>// Efek 3D ringan pada kartu hero.<br>const heroVisual = $(".hero-visual");<br>const heroCard = {<br>heroVisual.addEventListener("pointermove", event => {<br>const rect = heroVisual.getBoundingClientRect();<br>const x = (event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;<br>const y = (event.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height;<br>heroCard.style.transform =<br>`perspective(900px) rotateX(${(0.5 - y) * 9}deg) ` +<br>`rotateY(${(x - 0.5) * 9}deg) translateY(-3px)`;<br>});<br>heroVisual.addEventListener("pointerleave", () => {<br>heroCard.style.transform = "";<br>});<br>}<br>// ========================================<br>// MOOD CHECK-IN<br>// 5`;<br>intensityValue.setAttribute("aria-label", `Intensitas ${value} dari 5`);<br>intensity.style.setProperty(<br>"--range-progress",<br>`${((value - 1) / 4) * 100}%`<br>);<br>}<br>if (intensity) {<br>intensity.addEventListener("input", updateIntensity);<br>intensity.addEventListener("change", updateIntensity);<br>updateIntensity();<br>}<br>if (moodForm) Number(data.get("intensity") \|\| 3);<br>const factors = data.getAll("factors").map(String);<br>const note = String(data.get("note") \|\| "").trim();<br>if (!mood) {<br>showFeedback("mood-result", "Pilih mood terlebih dahulu.", true);<br>return;<br>}<br>const entry = {<br>id: Date.now().toString(),<br>mood,<br>intensity: value,<br>factors,<br>note,<br>createdAt: new Date().toISOString()<br>};<br>const previous = moods;<br>moods = [entry, ...moods].slice(0, 250);<br>if (!save(KEYS.moods, moods)) {<br>moods = previous;<br>return;<br>}<br>const messages = {<br>senang: "Nikmati hal baik yang sedang kamu rasakan.",<br>tenang: "Beri dirimu ruang untuk menikmati ketenangan ini.",<br>biasa: "Merasa biasa saja juga valid.",<br>cemas: "Coba ambil jeda dan fokus pada satu hal yang bisa kamu kendalikan.",<br>sedih: "Kamu tidak harus buru-buru menghilangkan rasa sedih.",<br>lelah: "Jika memungkinkan, beri dirimu waktu untuk beristirahat."<br>};<br>let message = messages[mood];<br>if (value >= 4 && ["cemas", "sedih", "lelah"].includes(mood)) {<br>message += " Kamu boleh berbagi dengan seseorang yang kamu percaya.";<br>}<br>showFeedback(<br>"mood-result",<br>`Mood ${moodNames[mood]} (${value}/5) berhasil disimpan. ${message}`<br>);<br>toast("Mood check-in berhasil disimpan.");<br>renderConstellation();<br>});<br>}<br>// ========================================<br>// REFLEKSI DIRI: 10 PERTANYAAN<br>// ========================================<br>const questions = [<br>"Seberapa sering kamu kehilangan minat pada kegiatan yang biasanya kamu sukai?",<br>"Seberapa sering kamu merasa sedih, kosong, atau putus asa?",<br>"Seberapa sering kamu merasa cemas atau sulit menghentikan pikiran yang berputar?",<br>"Seberapa sering kamu sulit tidur atau merasa lelah?",<br>"Seberapa sering perasaanmu membuat sekolah atau hubungan dengan orang lain terasa sulit?",<br>"Seberapa sering kamu kesulitan berkonsentrasi?",<br>"Seberapa sering kamu merasa kewalahan oleh tugas atau ekspektasi?",<br>"Seberapa sering kamu merasa kesepian atau tidak punya Array(10).fill(null);<br>const intro = $("#questionnaire-intro");<br>const questionnaire = $("#questionnaire-form");<br>const questionContainer = $("#question-container");<br>const questionBack = $("#question-back");<br>const questionNext = $("#question-next");<br>const questionSubmit = $("#question-submit");<br>const reflectionResult = $("#reflection-result");<br>function renderQuestion() {<br>if (!questionContainer) return;<br>const index = questionIndex;<br>questionContainer.innerHTML = `<br><fieldset class="question-card form-group"><br><legend class="question-title"><br>${index + Number(selected.value) : null;<br>}<br>function storeAnswer() {<br>const value = currentAnswer();<br>if (value === null) {<br>showFeedback(<br>"question-error",<br>"Pilih salah satu jawaban sebelum melanjutkan.",<br>true<br>);<br>return false;<br>}<br>answers[questionIndex] = value;<br>return true;<br>}<br>$("#start-questionnaire")?.addEventListener("click", () => {<br>intro.hidden = true;<br>questionnaire.hidden = false;<br>reflectionResult.hidden = true;<br>questionIndex = 0;<br>renderQuestion();<br>});<br>questionNext?.addEventListener("click", () => {<br>if (!storeAnswer()) return;<br>if (questionIndex < 9) {<br>questionIndex++;<br>renderQuestion();<br>}<br>});<br>questionBack?.addEventListener("click", () => {<br>const value = currentAnswer();<br>if (value !== null) answers[questionIndex] = value;<br>if (questionIndex > 0) {<br>questionIndex--;<br>renderQuestion();<br>}<br>});<br>questionnaire?.addEventListener("submit", event => {<br>event.preventDefault();<br>if (!storeAnswer()) return;<br>if (answers.some(answer => answer === null)) {<br>showFeedback("question-error", "Masih ada pertanyaan yang belum dijawab.", true);<br>return;<br>}<br>const scores = answers.slice(0, 9);<br>const average = scores.reduce((sum, score) => sum + score, 0) / 9;<br>let title;<br>let description;<br>if (average < 0.75) {<br>title = "Perasaanmu tampak relatif stabil belakangan ini.";<br>description = "Pengalaman yang ditanyakan tampaknya tidak terlalu sering muncul. Kamu bisa memulai dengan satu langkah kecil.";<br>} else {<br>title = "Kamu mungkin membutuhkan dukungan lebih.";<br>description = "Beberapa pengalaman ini mungkin cukup sering muncul. Ada satu hal penting: beberapa fitur baru bisa benar-benar berfungsi setelah JavaScript-nya dibuat, tetapi itu tidak menghalangi kita memperbaiki CSS sekarang.<br>Kita bikin ulang `style.css` berdasarkan HTML terbarumu, supaya revisinya nggak saling bertabrakan.<br>Konsep desain yang kita pakai:<br>* Background utama: dark navy, bukan putih.<br>* Section dan kartu: putih atau putih kebiruan agar kontras.<br>* Warna aksen: ungu, biru, mint, dan sedikit pink.<br>* Hero: visual mengambang dengan efek kedalaman 3D.<br>* Interaksi: hover, animasi lembut, efek fokus, dan responsif untuk HP maupun laptop.<br>* Kompatibel dengan HTML kamu: menggunakan nama class dan ID yang sudah ada.<br>### CSS Moodora — versi revisi<br>Buka file `style.css` di GitHub, hapus isi CSS lama, lalu 18px;<br>}<br>.section-description {<br>max-width: 650px;<br>color: #b9bfd5;<br>font-size: 1.02rem;<br>}<br>.section-heading {<br>margin-bottom: 38px;<br>}<br>.eyebrow {<br>display: !important;<br>transition-duration: .01ms !important;<br>}<br>}<br>```<br>### Cara memasangnya di GitHub<br>* Buka repository Moodora kamu.<br>* Buka file `style.css`, lalu tekan ikon pensil untuk mengedit.<br>* Hapus CSS versi lama dan tempel kode di atas.<br>* Tekan Commit changes.<br>* Buka website GitHub Pages dan refresh. Kalau tampilannya belum berubah, coba refresh paksa dengan `Ctrl + F5` di laptop.<br>Catatan penting: CSS ini mengubah tampilan semua section yang ada di HTML kamu, bukan hanya beranda. Namun, CSS hanya mengatur tampilan—intensitas mood yang selalu menunjukkan `3 / 5`, kuesioner, penyimpanan journal, dan interaksi lainnya tetap membutuhkan JavaScript yang sesuai.<br>Setelah CSS ini terpasang, kita bisa lanjut merapikan `script.js` agar cocok dengan ID di HTML terbaru kamu, sehingga tidak ada fitur yang rusak akibat revisi sebelumnya. | setelah code ini di pasangan ternyata ada beberapa tampilan yang kena border dan terlihat jelek | Chatgpt |
